@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 />

@layerwell/mobile:test: +             )}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           </Card>

@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         <Card className="mt-4">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           <Text variant="label" tone="muted">

@layerwell/mobile:test: +             disabled={busy !== null}

@layerwell/mobile:test: +           />
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           <Button

@layerwell/mobile:test: +             label={restore.isPending ? 'Checking purchases...' : 'Restore purchases'}

@layerwell/mobile:test: +           <Button

@layerwell/mobile:test: +   );
@layerwell/mobile:test: + }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: + export function HealthDataLifecycleGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const { user, initializing } = useAuth();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const segments = useSegments();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const ownerUserId =
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     user?.id ?? (!isSupabaseConfigured ? LOCAL_UNCONFIGURED_HEALTH_DATA_OWNER : null);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const [gateSnapshot, setGateSnapshot] = useState(createHealthDataGateSnapshot);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const gateSnapshotRef = useRef(gateSnapshot);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const reconciliationBaselineRef = useRef<{
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     token: symbol;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     record: HealthDataLifecycleRecord | null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   } | null>(null);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const activationReleasePromiseRef = useRef<Promise<void> | null>(null);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const record = gateSnapshot.record;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const [reconcileRevision, setReconcileRevision] = useState(0);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const activeLeaseAuthorityKey =
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     ownerUserId !== null && record?.ownerUserId === ownerUserId && record.state === 'active'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       ? processingLeaseAuthorityKey(ownerUserId, record.processingEpoch)
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       : null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const [leaseClosePendingAuthorityKey, setLeaseClosePendingAuthorityKey] = useState<string | null>(
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     null,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   );
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const leaseClosePending =
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     activeLeaseAuthorityKey !== null && leaseClosePendingAuthorityKey === activeLeaseAuthorityKey;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const leaseClosePromiseRef = useRef<{
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     authorityKey: string;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     promise: Promise<void>;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   } | null>(null);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const [activationReleaseFailureOwner, setActivationReleaseFailureOwner] = useState<string | null>(
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     null,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   );
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const commitLifecycleRecord = useCallback(
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     (next: HealthDataLifecycleRecord | null, transitionFrom?: HealthDataLifecycleRecord | null) => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const current = gateSnapshotRef.current;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const updated =
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         transitionFrom === undefined
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           ? applyHealthDataLifecycleRecord(current, next)
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           : applyHealthDataLifecycleRecord(current, next, transitionFrom);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       gateSnapshotRef.current = updated;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       setGateSnapshot(updated);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     },
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     [],
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   );
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const beginProcessingLeaseClose = useCallback(
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       scheduledLease: ActiveHealthProcessingLeaseSnapshot | null,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       processingEpoch: number,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     ): Promise<void> => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       if (!ownerUserId) return Promise.resolve();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const authorityKey = processingLeaseAuthorityKey(ownerUserId, processingEpoch);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const existing = leaseClosePromiseRef.current;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       if (existing?.authorityKey === authorityKey) return existing.promise;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       setLeaseClosePendingAuthorityKey(authorityKey);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const pending = (async () => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         try {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           await drainRoutineWidgetOutboxBeforeHealthLeaseClose({
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             ownerUserId,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             processingEpoch,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         } catch {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           // The drain closes native admission even on failure/timeout. Lease
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           // closure below remains mandatory and never waits beyond its bound.
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         const cleared = clearExactProcessingLease(scheduledLease, ownerUserId);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         if (!cleared) return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         const current = gateSnapshotRef.current.record;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         if (current?.ownerUserId === ownerUserId && current.state === 'active') {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           commitLifecycleRecord(verificationRequiredRecord({ ownerUserId, previous: current }));
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         setReconcileRevision((revision) => revision + 1);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       })();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const operation = { authorityKey, promise: pending };
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       leaseClosePromiseRef.current = operation;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const settle = () => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         if (leaseClosePromiseRef.current === operation) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           leaseClosePromiseRef.current = null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           setLeaseClosePendingAuthorityKey((current) =>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             current === authorityKey ? null : current,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           );
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       };
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       void pending.then(settle, settle);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       return pending;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     },
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     [commitLifecycleRecord, ownerUserId],
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   );
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   useEffect(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const operation = leaseClosePromiseRef.current;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (operation !== null && operation.authorityKey !== activeLeaseAuthorityKey) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       leaseClosePromiseRef.current = null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }, [activeLeaseAuthorityKey]);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   useEffect(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     clearActiveHealthProcessingEpoch();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     reconciliationBaselineRef.current = null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const empty = createHealthDataGateSnapshot();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     gateSnapshotRef.current = empty;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     activationReleasePromiseRef.current = null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     leaseClosePromiseRef.current = null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }, [ownerUserId]);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   useEffect(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     return subscribeToHealthDataLifecycle((next) => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       if (next === null || next.ownerUserId === ownerUserId) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         const baseline = reconciliationBaselineRef.current;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         reconciliationBaselineRef.current = null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         if (baseline) commitLifecycleRecord(next, baseline.record);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         else commitLifecycleRecord(next);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }, [commitLifecycleRecord, ownerUserId]);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const activationRouteSelected = healthDataActivationRouteIsSelected(segments);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   useEffect(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const current = gateSnapshotRef.current;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (current.activationRouteEpoch === null) return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (!activationRouteSelected) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       router.replace('/onboarding/goals');
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }, [activationRouteSelected, gateSnapshot.activationRouteEpoch]);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const acknowledgeMountedGoalsRoute = useCallback((): Promise<void> => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (activationReleasePromiseRef.current) return activationReleasePromiseRef.current;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const pending = (async () => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const current = gateSnapshotRef.current;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       if (current.activationRouteEpoch === null || current.record?.state !== 'active') return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const pendingOwnerUserId = current.record.ownerUserId;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const pendingEpoch = current.activationRouteEpoch;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       setActivationReleaseFailureOwner(null);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       try {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         const next = await completeHealthDataActivationRoute({
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           ownerUserId: pendingOwnerUserId,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           expectedProcessingEpoch: pendingEpoch,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         const liveRecord = gateSnapshotRef.current.record;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         if (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           liveRecord?.ownerUserId !== pendingOwnerUserId ||
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           liveRecord.processingEpoch !== pendingEpoch
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         ) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         commitLifecycleRecord(next);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         const latest = gateSnapshotRef.current;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         const released = releaseHealthDataActivationRoute(latest, ['onboarding', 'goals']);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         if (released !== latest) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           gateSnapshotRef.current = released;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           setGateSnapshot(released);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       } catch {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         const live = gateSnapshotRef.current;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         if (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           live.record?.ownerUserId === pendingOwnerUserId &&
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           live.activationRouteEpoch === pendingEpoch
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         ) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           setActivationReleaseFailureOwner(pendingOwnerUserId);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     })();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     activationReleasePromiseRef.current = pending;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     void pending.finally(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       if (activationReleasePromiseRef.current === pending) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         activationReleasePromiseRef.current = null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     return pending;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }, [commitLifecycleRecord]);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const unconsentedRouteAllowed = unconsentedRouteMayMount(segments);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const signedOutRouteAllowed = signedOutConfiguredRouteMayMount(segments);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   useEffect(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (!initializing && isSupabaseConfigured && ownerUserId === null && !signedOutRouteAllowed) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       router.replace('/');
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }, [initializing, ownerUserId, signedOutRouteAllowed]);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   useEffect(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const redirectRequired = healthDataUnconsentedRedirectRequired(
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       gateSnapshotRef.current,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       ownerUserId,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       unconsentedRouteAllowed,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     );
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (redirectRequired) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       router.replace('/onboarding/consent');
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }, [ownerUserId, record?.ownerUserId, record?.state, unconsentedRouteAllowed]);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   useEffect(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (!ownerUserId) return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     let active = true;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const reconcile = async () => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       let next: HealthDataLifecycleRecord;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const before = await readHealthDataLifecycle(ownerUserId).catch(() => null);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const baselineToken = Symbol('health-data-reconcile');
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       reconciliationBaselineRef.current = { token: baselineToken, record: before };
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       try {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         next = await reconcileHealthDataLifecycle(ownerUserId);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         if (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           next.state === 'withdrawing' ||
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           (next.state === 'withdrawn' && !next.localCleanupComplete)
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         ) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           next = await resumeHealthDataConsentWithdrawal(ownerUserId);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       } catch (error) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         if (isAccountGenerationLeaseError(error)) return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         next =
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           (await readHealthDataLifecycle(ownerUserId).catch(() => null)) ??
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           verificationRequiredRecord({ ownerUserId });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       } finally {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         if (reconciliationBaselineRef.current?.token === baselineToken) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           reconciliationBaselineRef.current = null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       if (active) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         commitLifecycleRecord(next, before);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     };
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     void reconcile();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const appState = AppState.addEventListener('change', (state) => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       if (state === 'active') {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         const current = gateSnapshotRef.current.record;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         if (current?.ownerUserId !== ownerUserId || current.state !== 'active') return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         void beginProcessingLeaseClose(
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           activeHealthProcessingLeaseSnapshot(ownerUserId),
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           current.processingEpoch,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         );
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     return () => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       active = false;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       appState.remove();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     };
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }, [beginProcessingLeaseClose, commitLifecycleRecord, ownerUserId, reconcileRevision]);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   useEffect(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (!isSupabaseConfigured || record?.ownerUserId !== ownerUserId || record.state !== 'active') {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const expiresAt = record.serverVerifiedAt
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       ? healthProcessingStatusLeaseExpiresAt(record.serverVerifiedAt)
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       : null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const delay =
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       expiresAt === null
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         ? 0
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         : Math.max(0, expiresAt - ROUTINE_WIDGET_RECONCILIATION_HEADROOM_MS - Date.now());
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const scheduledLease = activeHealthProcessingLeaseSnapshot(ownerUserId);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const timer = setTimeout(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       const current = gateSnapshotRef.current.record;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       if (current?.ownerUserId !== ownerUserId || current.state !== 'active') return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       void beginProcessingLeaseClose(scheduledLease, current.processingEpoch);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     }, delay);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     return () => clearTimeout(timer);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }, [beginProcessingLeaseClose, ownerUserId, record]);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   if (initializing) return null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   // The Welcome route must be able to create the first anonymous account.
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   if (!ownerUserId) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (isSupabaseConfigured && !signedOutRouteAllowed) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       return (

@layerwell/mobile:test: +   motionAllowed,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   useReduceMotionPreference,
@layerwell/mobile:test: + } from '@/lib/accessibility/useReduceMotionPreference';
@layerwell/mobile:test: + import { track } from '@/lib/analytics/track';
@layerwell/mobile:test: + import { colors } from '@/theme/tokens';
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: + const ONBOARDING_PRODUCT_TARGET = 3;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: + function CategoryPickerSheet({
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   visible,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   selectedCategory,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   onSelect,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   onClose,
@layerwell/mobile:test: + }: {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   visible: boolean;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   selectedCategory: ProductCategory | null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   onSelect: (category: ProductCategory | null) => void;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   onClose: () => void;
@layerwell/mobile:test: + }) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const { height: viewportHeight } = useWindowDimensions();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const sheetMaxHeight = Math.max(0, viewportHeight - 52);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const sheetPaddingBottom = insets.bottom > 0 ? Math.max(40, insets.bottom + 24) : undefined;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   if (!visible) return null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   return (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     <View
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       className="absolute inset-0 justify-end"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       style={{ backgroundColor: 'rgba(32,27,21,0.4)', zIndex: 20, elevation: 20 }}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       <View className="flex-1 justify-end">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         <Pressable
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           className="flex-1"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           accessibilityLabel="Dismiss category picker"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           accessibilityRole="button"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           onPress={onClose}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         />
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         <View
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           aria-modal
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           role="dialog"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           accessibilityLabel="Choose product category"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           accessibilityViewIsModal
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           className="overflow-hidden rounded-t-sheet bg-paper px-6 pb-10 pt-4"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           style={
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             sheetPaddingBottom === undefined
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               ? { height: sheetMaxHeight, maxHeight: sheetMaxHeight }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               : {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   height: sheetMaxHeight,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   maxHeight: sheetMaxHeight,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   paddingBottom: sheetPaddingBottom,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           <View
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             className="mx-auto mb-4 h-[5px] w-10 rounded-[3px]"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             style={{ backgroundColor: 'rgba(32,27,21,0.15)' }}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           />
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           <View className="mb-4 flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             <Text variant="titleSm" className="flex-1">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               Product category
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             <Pressable
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               accessibilityLabel="Close category picker"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               accessibilityRole="button"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               className="min-h-[48px] min-w-[64px] items-center justify-center rounded-pill px-3"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               onPress={onClose}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               <Text variant="bodySm" className="font-sans-semibold" style={{ color: colors.clay }}>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 Close
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             </Pressable>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           <ScrollView
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             style={{ flexShrink: 1 }}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             contentContainerClassName="pb-6"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             <View className="flex-row flex-wrap gap-2">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               {ONBOARDING_PRODUCT_CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 <Chip
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   key={c.id}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   label={c.label}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   selected={selectedCategory === c.id}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   className="px-4"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   onPress={() => onSelect(selectedCategory === c.id ? null : c.id)}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 />
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               ))}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           </ScrollView>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   );
@layerwell/mobile:test: + }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: + // 06 · Current products intake (docs/01 §2 step 6, docs/04 §4.7). Seeds the shelf with
@layerwell/mobile:test: + // addedVia:'onboarding' so the reveal and the first routine/conflict pass are built
@layerwell/mobile:test: + // from the user's REAL products (the conflict engine tags off the name). Skip stays
@layerwell/mobile:test: + // visible. The full barcode/OCR intake lives on the Shelf (docs/04); this is the
@layerwell/mobile:test: + // lightweight first-population that was previously a dead skip-only screen.
@layerwell/mobile:test: + export default function ProductsScreen() {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const { addedProductId } = useLocalSearchParams<{ addedProductId?: string }>();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   return (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     <ShelfDataAvailabilityGate>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       <ProductsScreenContent key={addedProductId ?? 'initial-add'} />
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     </ShelfDataAvailabilityGate>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   );
@layerwell/mobile:test: + }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: + function ProductsScreenContent() {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const { addedProductId } = useLocalSearchParams<{ addedProductId?: string }>();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const reduceMotion = useReduceMotionPreference();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const { fontScale = 1, height, width } = useWindowDimensions();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const scrollRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const inputRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const { goals } = useOnboarding();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const { reset: resetIntake } = useIntake();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const { data } = useShelf();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const m = useShelfMutations();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const [name, setName] = useState('');
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const [category, setCategory] = useState<ProductCategory | null>(null);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const [categoryPickerOpen, setCategoryPickerOpen] = useState(false);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const added = data?.items ?? [];
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const supportFloorTextPressurePhone =
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     width <= 430 && height >= 640 && height < 700 && (fontScale >= 1.3 || Platform.OS === 'web');
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const modernTextPressurePhone =
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     width <= 390 && height >= 800 && height < 900 && (fontScale >= 1.3 || Platform.OS === 'web');
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const splitShortPhone = height < 460;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const compactPhone =
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     height < 640 || supportFloorTextPressurePhone || modernTextPressurePhone || splitShortPhone;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const showIntroCopy = !modernTextPressurePhone && !splitShortPhone;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const showProgressBody = !modernTextPressurePhone && !splitShortPhone;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const compactFooterAdds = compactPhone && name.trim().length > 0;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const showCompactCategoryFooter = compactFooterAdds;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const remainingToTarget = Math.max(ONBOARDING_PRODUCT_TARGET - added.length, 0);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const hasTargetProducts = remainingToTarget === 0;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const showContinueAnyway = added.length > 0 && !hasTargetProducts && !compactFooterAdds;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const productsBottomPaddingClass =
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     splitShortPhone && showContinueAnyway && added.length === 1
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       ? 'pb-44'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       : showContinueAnyway
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         ? 'pb-36'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         : 'pb-28';
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const remainingProductNoun = remainingToTarget === 1 ? 'product' : 'products';
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const continueAnywayLabel = `Continue with ${added.length} ${
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     added.length === 1 ? 'product' : 'products'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }`;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const footerPrimaryLabel = compactFooterAdds
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     ? 'Add to shelf'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     : hasTargetProducts
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       ? 'Continue'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       : added.length > 0
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         ? `Add ${remainingToTarget} more`
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         : 'Skip for now';
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const progressBody = hasTargetProducts
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     ? 'Good. That is enough for a stronger first routine, and you can still add more later.'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     : added.length > 0
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       ? `${remainingToTarget} more ${remainingProductNoun} gives your first insight more to work with.`
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       : 'Three products gives your first routine enough context to spot useful gaps or timing notes.';
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   const selectedCategoryLabel =
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     ONBOARDING_PRODUCT_CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.id === category)?.label ?? null;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   useEffect(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     trackProductAddStarted('onboarding');
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }, []);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   useEffect(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (!addedProductId || added.length === 0) return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       scrollRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: false });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }, [added.length, addedProductId]);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   function focusNextProduct() {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     scrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: motionAllowed(reduceMotion) });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     requestAnimationFrame(() => inputRef.current?.focus());
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   function selectCategory(nextCategory: ProductCategory | null) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     setCategory(nextCategory);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     setCategoryPickerOpen(false);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   function add() {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const trimmed = name.trim();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (!trimmed) return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     const intakeId = resetIntake({
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       name: trimmed,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       category,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       addedVia: 'onboarding',
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       catalogSource: 'user_local',
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       catalogMatchQuality: 'manual',
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       paoMonths: null,
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       paoSource: 'unknown',
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     setCategoryPickerOpen(false);
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     router.push({ pathname: '/shelf/opened', params: { intakeId, origin: 'onboarding' } });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   function go() {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (goals.length === 0) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       router.replace('/onboarding/goals');
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     track('screen_viewed', { screen_name: 'products_intake', count: added.length });
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     router.push('/onboarding/analyzing');
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   function footerAction() {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (compactFooterAdds) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       add();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     if (added.length === 0 || hasTargetProducts) {
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       go();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       return;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     focusNextProduct();
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +   return (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +     <Screen>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       <View
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         className="flex-1 overflow-hidden"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         aria-hidden={categoryPickerOpen || undefined}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         accessibilityElementsHidden={categoryPickerOpen}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         importantForAccessibility={categoryPickerOpen ? 'no-hide-descendants' : 'auto'}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         style={{ minHeight: 0 }}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         <ScrollView
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           ref={scrollRef}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           className="flex-1"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           contentContainerClassName={productsBottomPaddingClass}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           <Text
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             variant="title"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             className={splitShortPhone ? 'mt-3' : compactPhone ? 'mt-4' : 'mt-6'}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             style={splitShortPhone ? { fontSize: 27, lineHeight: 30 } : undefined}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             What&apos;s on your shelf?
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           {showIntroCopy ? (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             <Text variant="body" tone="muted" className={compactPhone ? 'mt-2' : 'mt-3'}>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               Add the products you already use so we build around them. Even just the name helps us
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               spot conflicts. You can add more anytime from your Shelf.
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           ) : null}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           <View
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             className={
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               splitShortPhone
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 ? 'mt-3 rounded-2xl bg-greige-chip px-4 py-2.5'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 : compactPhone
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   ? 'mt-3 rounded-2xl bg-greige-chip px-4 py-3'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   : 'mt-4 rounded-2xl bg-greige-chip px-4 py-3'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             style={{ borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.hairline }}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             <Text variant="label" tone="clay">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               {Math.min(added.length, ONBOARDING_PRODUCT_TARGET)} OF {ONBOARDING_PRODUCT_TARGET}{' '}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               PRODUCTS
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             {showProgressBody ? (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               <Text variant="bodySm" tone="muted" className="mt-1">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 {progressBody}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             ) : null}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           <Card
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             className={
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               splitShortPhone
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 ? 'mt-3 p-4'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 : modernTextPressurePhone
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   ? 'mt-3 p-4'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   : compactPhone
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     ? 'mt-4 p-4'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     : 'mt-6'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             <Text variant="label" tone="muted" className="mb-2">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               PRODUCT NAME
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             <TextInput
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               ref={inputRef}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               accessibilityLabel="Product name"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               maxLength={SHELF_PRODUCT_NAME_MAX_LENGTH}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               value={name}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               onChangeText={setName}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               placeholder="e.g. Retinol serum"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               placeholderTextColor={colors.mutedLight}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               className="rounded-card border border-hairline bg-paper px-4 py-3.5 font-sans text-base text-ink"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               returnKeyType="done"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               onSubmitEditing={add}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             />
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             {!compactPhone ? (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               <>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 <Text variant="label" tone="muted" className="mb-2 mt-4">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   CATEGORY · OPTIONAL
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 <View className="flex-row flex-wrap gap-2">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   {ONBOARDING_PRODUCT_CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     <Chip
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       key={c.id}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       label={c.label}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       selected={category === c.id}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       onPress={() => setCategory(category === c.id ? null : c.id)}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     />
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   ))}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               </>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             ) : null}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             {!compactPhone ? (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               <Button
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 className="mt-4"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 label="Add to shelf"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 variant="inverse"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 disabled={!name.trim()}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 onPress={add}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               />
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             ) : null}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           </Card>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           {added.length > 0 ? (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             <View className="mt-5">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               <Text variant="label" tone="muted" className="mb-2">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 {added.length} ON YOUR SHELF
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               <View className="gap-2">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 {added.map((it) => (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   <View
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     key={it.id}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     className="flex-row items-center justify-between rounded-card bg-paper-raised px-4 py-3"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     style={{ borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.hairline }}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     <Text variant="body" className="flex-1 font-sans-medium">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       {it.name}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     <Pressable
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       accessibilityRole="button"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       accessibilityLabel={`Remove ${it.name}`}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       onPress={() => void m.remove(it.id)}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       className="h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       style={({ pressed }) => (pressed ? { opacity: 0.72 } : undefined)}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       <Text variant="body" tone="muted" style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                         ×
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                       </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                     </Pressable>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                   </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 ))}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           ) : null}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         </ScrollView>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       {compactPhone ? (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         <CategoryPickerSheet
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           visible={categoryPickerOpen}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           selectedCategory={category}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           onSelect={selectCategory}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           onClose={() => setCategoryPickerOpen(false)}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         />
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       ) : null}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       <View
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         className="bg-paper pb-4 pt-2"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         aria-hidden={categoryPickerOpen || undefined}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         accessibilityElementsHidden={categoryPickerOpen}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         importantForAccessibility={categoryPickerOpen ? 'no-hide-descendants' : 'auto'}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +       >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         {showCompactCategoryFooter ? (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           <Pressable
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             accessibilityRole="button"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             accessibilityLabel={
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               selectedCategoryLabel
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 ? `Category, ${selectedCategoryLabel}`
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 : 'Choose product category'
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             }
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             onPress={() => setCategoryPickerOpen((open) => !open)}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             className="mb-2 min-h-[48px] flex-row items-center justify-between rounded-card border border-hairline bg-paper-raised px-4 py-3"
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             style={({ pressed }) => (pressed ? { opacity: 0.82 } : undefined)}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           >
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             <View className="flex-1 pr-3">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               <Text variant="label" tone="muted">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 CATEGORY · OPTIONAL
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               <Text variant="body" className="mt-0.5 font-sans-medium">
@layerwell/mobile:test: +                 {selectedCategoryLabel ?? 'Choose category'}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             </View>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             <Text variant="body" tone="muted" style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +               &gt;
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             </Text>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           </Pressable>
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         ) : null}
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         <Button label={footerPrimaryLabel} onPress={footerAction} />
@layerwell/mobile:test: +         {showContinueAnyway ? (
@layerwell/mobile:test: +           <Button
@layerwell/mobile:test: +             label={continueAnywayLabel}
